## This Week in Security: Your News Briefing

Welcome to your weekly security roundup. We've tracked down the **10 most important stories** this
week—the ones everyone's talking about, from critical threats to emerging trends that could shape
your security posture. Leading the news this week is **CVE-2026-114432**, which has sparked
conversation across 7 sources. Meanwhile, the industry is closely tracking **Threat Modeling and
Social Issues** with 4 mentions, along with emerging details on **Hackers exploit SSHole OpenSSH
flaw to breach Linux systems**, **CVE-2026-123796 | tmatens compose-lint up to 0.32.x
information...**, and **CVE-2026-123373 | tmatens compose-lint up to 0.24.x YAML Parser...**. Here's
the full breakdown of what you need to know.

First, the stories that demand your immediate attention:

**1. CVE-2026-114432**

   [Get the details →](https://shostack.org/blog/threat-modeling-and-social-issues/)

**3. Hackers exploit SSHole OpenSSH flaw to breach Linux systems**

   [Get the details →](https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/hackers-exploit-sshole-openssh-flaw-to-breach-linux-systems/)

### 🛠️ Tools, Updates & Releases

New capabilities and releases worth knowing about:

**1. Enterprises must take blame for their AI agents’ mistakes, say regulators**
   The UK’s digital watchdogs have warned enterprises that they can’t evade their legal responsibilitie. Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://www.computerworld.com/article/4245769/enterprises-must-take-blame-for-their-ai-agents-mistakes-say-regulators-2.html)

**2. CrowdStrike launches 'AI kill switch' it claims can stop rogue agents and more**
   The new tool comes as part of Falcon Guardian, CrowdStrike's AI Detection and Response platform. Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://www.techradar.com/pro/security/crowdstrike-launches-ai-kill-switch-it-claims-can-stop-rogue-agents-and-more)

    <p class="text-muted-foreground mb-3 leading-relaxed">In November 2025, Harvard University suffered a data breach later linked to a ShinyHunters &quot;pay or leak&quot; extortion attempt . The data contained 851k unique email addresses relating to the university&#x27;s alumni and fundraising systems, along with extensive profile information for donors, alumni, par...</p>
